**92. Float fix 1 words (Marketing `2aff6193`, `docs/marketing/float-fix1-words-2026-10-13.md`; Design's code at `101f4a57`, `floatBound.ts` `FILED_COUNT_RULE`, `FILED_COUNT_WORDS`, `filedCountFace`; the live page `docs/public/are-float-quotes-real.md` at `2fa909bf`) against 89e, seventh tenure, 2026-10-13 (UTC).** Opened all of them; I did not run the board.

- **Strings 1 to 12: PASS against 89e.** No "latest", "current" or "float" for the count in 2, 4 to 8, 11 or 12; 3 says the true count may be higher and the code adds it only for the issuance reasons; 4 is the case plainly (it is never false: "may be" holds for a found split and an unproven one); 8 drops "latest"; 9 and 10 are true and 9 stands only on the filed-count panel (read 91's label stays on the bound and refused panels). Two advisories, no change required: (a) the reason sentence that opens "Cannot verify:" stays under the count face; Design is right that the first line ("It is not a TMI Float") makes it read as about the TMI Float, and I keep it. For COUNT_TOO_OLD the stored sentence ("the latest filed share count is from {date}, too old to rely on") and string 7 say the same thing twice; drop one if the hover is long. (b) The legend's preceding sentence says "The date beside the number is the filed share count's", and this face carries no date beside the number (it is in the hover); sentence 12 follows it, so a reader may look for the date: acceptable, but "the date is in the hover" would close it.
- **The code's set of faces with the word and no count** (Design's list, which matches my own read of `filedCountFace`): BOUND_OUT_OF_RANGE; a count dated after the board (`AFTER_DATE`); a row with no filed count (in practice FILINGS_NOT_REREAD on a row that has none, since `floatCellAt` returns it before the count is looked at); the no-reason fault (a float read that threw, or a malformed payload; the hover is the generic "Cannot verify."); a count whose date does not parse; a count of shares that is missing or not above zero. **REQUIRED: the page's narrowed "Cannot verify" bullet must not say "the two cases".** It leaves out "we have no filed count to show" and "we could not read the float data". Word it as the face with no count shown, with causes by example: no filed count to show, the float data could not be read, the figure did not pass our sanity check, or the count is dated after the board; the reason is in the detail.
- **REQUIRED: the new bullet's circled-i sentence is wider than the code.** The code sets the circled i (`caveat`) wherever the split check found none and the age is within 90 days, for any printed reason, including FILINGS_NOT_REREAD, whose hover says the desk has not read the filings since a date and adds no "may be higher" line (that line is set for the issuance reasons only). "A circled i means shares may have been issued since, so the true count may be higher" is false for that case. Word it as: the count passed the desk's split and age checks, the reason it could not be verified is in the hover, and where shares may have been issued since, the true count may be higher. The triangle sentence must also include "or we have no record of the check" (string 6), which the code sets as a warning.
- **REQUIRED: other sentences on the live page become false when the face ships and are not in Marketing's four changes.** (i) The introduction: "When it cannot be computed it says Unknown, Cannot verify or Not computed, and gives the reason" omits the filed count. (ii) "What a number does not tell you": "the name reads Cannot verify" (twice, for issuance and for a Form 20-F or 40-F count) is no longer what a visitor sees; it shows the filed count with an icon, or Cannot verify where no count can be shown. (iii) "What this is, and is not": "Where we show a number, it is computed from filings, and ... it is the most the float can be" is false of the filed count; say "Where we show a ceiling" and add that the filed count shown in its place is not a ceiling and not a float.
- **PASS: the first bullet's change** ("The TMI Float is no more than that, when the filings line up."; the defined term, read 67). Two other places on the page still carry the hedge "taking float as shares outstanding less insider holdings" (the last sentence of "How we do it" and the last paragraph); they are true, and a sweep for consistency is advisable.
- **PASS with a note: the landing sentence.** "Where the desk cannot verify one, it shows the company's filed share count with an icon, or Unknown, Cannot verify or Not computed, each with its reason." is true. The sentence (and both canvases, `Main.dc.html` and `LandingMobile.dc.html`) is in `lib/public/landingCopy.ts:139` on `origin/production` and `2fa909bf`; `origin/main` does not carry it (a search for the sentence on `origin/main` finds only this file), so the edit is made on the branch that carries it and shipped with the face.

**92a. The corrected "Cannot verify" bullet (Marketing `7e6b961f`), seventh tenure, 2026-10-13 (UTC).** "Cannot verify. We will not stand behind a number, and we show no count: the figure did not pass our own sanity check, the count is dated after the board or report it would sit on, we have no filed count to show, or we could not read the float data." Checked against `filedCountFace` at `101f4a57`: the four causes cover every path that returns the word with no count (BOUND_OUT_OF_RANGE; an AFTER_DATE count; no usable count, which takes in no count, a count of shares missing or not above zero, a count date that does not parse, and FILINGS_NOT_REREAD on a row with none; the no-reason fault and a malformed payload). **PASS, the first required change of read 92.** The other required changes of read 92 stand: the circled-i and triangle sentences, and the introduction, "What a number does not tell you" and "What this is, and is not".

**93. The revised float page passages (Marketing `45817b0b`, `docs/marketing/float-page-fix1-text-2026-10-13.md`; Research's `countChecksAt` at `ba6afbba`), seventh tenure, 2026-10-13 (UTC).** Opened the passages and `lib/research/floatRead.ts` at `ba6afbba`.

- **A correction to read 92 first, mine.** Read 92 said the circled i (`caveat`) is set "for any printed reason, including FILINGS_NOT_REREAD", so "a circled i means shares may have been issued since" was false there. I had not opened how `countChecks` is computed. `countChecksAt` returns `CHECKS_NOT_RUN` (both checks NOT_RUN) for a row that is not reused, which is exactly FILINGS_NOT_REREAD, so that reason always takes the triangle, never the circled i. The circled i needs split NONE and age WITHIN, which only the reasons the issuance code returns after the checks can have (ISSUANCE_FILED, ISSUANCE_FLAGGED, ISSUANCE_NOT_CHECKED, UNREGISTERED_SALE_NOT_CHECKABLE), and all four are in `ISSUANCE_REASONS`, so the hover always adds "the true share count may be higher". The original sentence was therefore true, and my required change was not needed. Marketing's new wording is also true; it is vaguer than the code allows, so the clearer true form is: "A circled i means the count passed the split and age checks but shares may have been issued since, so the true count may be higher." Either may stand. The triangle sentence is complete against the code: split FOUND or UNPROVEN, split NOT_RUN, a missing record, and age TOO_OLD; an age NOT_RUN adds no age sentence and rides with split NOT_RUN.
- **PASS:** passage 1 (the introduction), 2 ("the TMI Float is at most what is left"), 3 (the five things, the first bullet, the new bullet's triangle, the Unknown and Not computed bullets unchanged, the four-cause "Cannot verify"), and 4's first sentence (issuance: "shows the company's filed share count with an icon, or reads Cannot verify where no count can show").
- **REQUIRED, one tail (Marketing's question on 20-F and 40-F):** the code does not guarantee a Form 20-F or 40-F count is never a no-count case. The no-count paths (a count of shares missing or not above zero, an unparsable count date, a count dated after the board, a failed read) have no form condition. So the second sentence of passage 4 takes the same tail as the first: "... and the cell shows the filed share count with an icon, or reads Cannot verify where no count can show."
- **Advisory, passage 5:** "A filed share count shown with an icon is not a ceiling and not a float" is true as a statement of what the desk offers, but literally a count with nothing subtracted is at least the float on its date; "is not offered as a ceiling or as a float" says what is meant.
- **RULED, the two passed sentences left:** "We start with the latest filed share count we have for the company" (method, qualified by "we have") and "The figure is only as current as the filings under it" (a caution) both stay; 89e bars "latest" and "current" for the count the cell shows, and these describe the method and warn.

**94. The read 82 gate on the Claude checker (Research `d2162a43`, `result-claude.md`; raw `69909829`), seventh tenure, 2026-10-13 (UTC).** Opened the result and re-counted the raw records (`raw-claude/records.json`, `assignment.json`) myself rather than taking the table. **Verified:** 32 checks, all PUBLISHED, model `claude-opus-5-5`, cost $4.914; every one of the 17 gate seeds (the 14 counted, valuation, suitability and risk-reward, and the 3 in-sample originals the terra checker missed) was struck by an ADVICE or FORECAST answer in all 3 runs, none of them only by UNSUPPORTED; across the 210 must-pass-type line-runs (46 deal lines, 6 sample lines, 5 price facts and 13 read-75 lines, 3 runs each) there is not one ADVICE or FORECAST strike; the 8 placebo drafts carry none. **PASS, as registered in reads 82, 87a, 88 and 88a.** The pre-registered rule asked for the gate before the word list is switched off, and it is met on the Claude checker.

**Three things before and after the switch-off, in order of weight.** (1) **The fallback is unmeasured and will fire.** The production build keeps the OpenAI checker as the fallback for a failed Claude call (the 10-12 test saw 1 of 6 Opus calls fail), and the OpenAI checker is the one that missed 3 of 61 on the first run under the old question; it has not been run on the widened question. With word strikes off, the first report whose Claude call fails is checked by it. Either run the same set on it once (Research sized about $1.60) so the fallback's number is known, or leave the fallback on the word list. This needs deciding before the word list comes out, and the cost is his. (2) **The seeds are Research's, and the questions are mine.** The set is the author's own test; the check that does not share that blindness is the 30-day nightly scan by the other model (reads 82 (iii)), with struck text kept and a one-change switch back to the word list. It must ship in the same release as the switch-off, and I want to see where the restore switch lives. (3) **Advisory, the dash replacement (`dashes.ts`):** the rule " to " between a figure that ends in a digit or percent and one that starts with a digit or dollar is right for "$1 - $20" and wrong for a clause such as "gained 20% - 30 minutes later" (it would read "20% to 30 minutes later"). It is rare and the replaced text is rechecked, but the diagnostics already list every changed line: review every " to " replacement in the first week of live reports.

**For him, in plain words (through Integration):** the independent reader struck all 17 test lines of advice and price opinion in every run and wrongly struck none of the 52 real lines; it runs on Claude; if the Claude call fails a different model steps in that has not been tested with the new question (about $1.60 to test it); and every stored report is scanned each night for 30 days by the other model, with a switch back to the word list.
